Model before rewriting:

find x: int(5..20)
find y: int(0..5)
find z: int(0..6)

such that

x % y % z = 3

--

x
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x
new variables:
  find x#sign_magnitude_1: bool
  find x#sign_magnitude_2: bool
  find x#sign_magnitude_3: bool
  find x#sign_magnitude_4: bool
  find x#sign_magnitude_5: bool
  find x#sign_magnitude_6: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(x#sign_magnitude_5),and([!(x#sign_magnitude_4),or([!(x#sign_magnitude_3),and([!(x#sign_magnitude_2),and([!(x#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
  !(x#sign_magnitude_6)
  (!(x#sign_magnitude_6)) -> (or([!(x#sign_magnitude_5),and([!(x#sign_magnitude_4),or([!(x#sign_magnitude_3),and([!(x#sign_magnitude_2),and([!(x#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]))

  or([and([SATInt(SignMagnitude, [x#sign_magnitude_1,x#sign_magnitude_2,x#sign_magnitude_3,x#sign_magnitude_4,x#sign_magnitude_5,x#sign_magnitude_6;int(1..)] [5, 20]) >= 5,SATInt(SignMagnitude, [x#sign_magnitude_1,x#sign_magnitude_2,x#sign_magnitude_3,x#sign_magnitude_4,x#sign_magnitude_5,x#sign_magnitude_6;int(1..)] [5, 20]) <= 20;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
